# Feature Implementation Plan
**Feature:** ui-refresh (a visual redesign of Clinic Scribe's desktop app — a design-token theme with a Windows-following dark variant, one Review screen in place of the Transcript and Note tabs, a four-entry navigation rail with the setup screens behind a Settings gear, and the wording that names renamed screens — every privacy-load-bearing UI rule kept — shipped as 0.4.0; the first-run setup checklist, the full Review composition and the Chrome side panel follow as 0.4.1)
**Overall Progress:** `63%` (36 of 57 tasks; Phase M — eight tasks, M.0–M.7, merging `main`'s 0.3.4–0.3.7 into this branch and landing it — ADDED 2026-11-17 at `/review-plan`, see "Reconciliation — 2026-11-17"; before that 73 %, 36 of 49; Task 5.4 (packaging smoke) DONE 2026-10-21 — the local build at `f5bb91b` PASS; Phase S — eleven smoke-fix tasks, S.1–S.11 — DONE 2026-10-21: the suite green and the practitioner's live re-check PASS; Phases 1–5 and hardening H1–H4 done 2026-10-17; the live smokes of Phases 1–4 PASS through Phase S; decision 0.3, H5, Phase P and Phase 6 remain)

## Goal
### Why this plan
The app was built function-first over PLAN.md Phases 1–7; its nine tabs are stock Qt widgets with no theme, no spacing system and no visual hierarchy, and the everyday flow (Session → Transcript → Note) is mixed in with four setup tabs. The practitioner (2026-10-09): it "looks pretty messy and old", "a lot of stuff can get moved to a settings button", it "looks very confusing and in your face when you first open the app", and "when finishing a note through the app I have to complete it in a separate tab".

### What the practitioner decided (2026-10-09, this planning session)
- Scope: the look, the layout and the wording; a FULL redesign is acceptable mid-pilot ("I only have 4 patients left for today and then it's the weekend").
- Theme: light AND dark, following the Windows app-mode setting.
- Direction: mockup **A — "Clinic"** (light canvas, blue accent, Segoe UI Variable, a left icon rail, cards; a dark variant of the same tokens). Mockups B ("Paper") and C ("Console") were shown and not chosen.
- The everyday flow: **Record → Review**. One Review screen — transcript left, note right, one button row (Save note → Write draft to Cliniko → Copy note; Complete → Discard on the right), "Edit the note" as a strip under the note that opens the existing line tools; before a note exists the right pane holds today's "Generate note" group.
- Settings: all four setup tabs (Microphone, Practitioner, Clinics, Status) behind a Settings gear, as a view inside the main window; the main window shows Record, Review, Past sessions and Recovery.
- First open: a one-time **setup checklist** page (microphone ✓, clinic key ✓, voice profile ✓, Chrome link ✓) that goes away once done; recording keeps working without the voice profile.
- The Chrome side panel: **matched later** — a final optional phase, same tokens, no behaviour change, after the desktop build is installed and settled.

### What the practitioner decided at `/review-plan` (2026-10-09, the same session)
- 0.4.0 ships Phases 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 in THIS plan's numbering: theme, Review screen, shell, the wording for renamed screens, docs/version — the checklist, the full Review composition (the one shadow-exit pin change) and the Chrome panel go to 0.4.1 (Phase 6), because the checklist never shows on a set-up machine and the weekend install is the target.
- Rail icons: painter primitives drawn in code (decision 0.4 resolved — no QtSvg, no bundling branch).
- The header strip hides the live patient line while the Past sessions page is current, so "Hide names" keeps its meaning there.
- Wording: only lines naming RENAMED screens change (Session → Record; Transcript/Note → Review; "Recovery tab" → "Recovery page"); the ~40 lines saying "the Clinics / Status / Practitioner / Microphone tab" stay verbatim because the Settings strip's four tabs keep exactly those titles.
- Accepted: a compact density is a non-goal; a title bar that does not follow dark mode at the Phase 1 smoke is a follow-up (`windows:darkmode=2`), not a 0.4.0 fix.

### What done looks like
A build of record 0.4.0 whose desktop app opens on a calm Record screen with the rail and header strip, the Review screen and the Settings strip, in light or dark as Windows is set — with `desktop/tests/test_shadow_exits.py` passing UNCHANGED, every other privacy and interaction rule in `docs/design-system.md` intact, that document's new "Tokens" section pointing at the theme module, and the practitioner's live smokes passed on the developer build; installed over a weekend after every recording is Completed or Discarded, with the clinic 1 smoke continuing on it. Then 0.4.1: the setup checklist, the full Review composition (its one named pin change) and, if P.4 says so, the Chrome panel.

### Excluded — Revisit Only If Needed
- Any change to what the app records, keeps, writes or copies; any change to a consent text, the retention warning, the write lines, the refusal lines' substance or the Chrome reason CODES — the redesign is presentation only. A refusal, hotkey or Chrome line that names a RENAMED screen is reworded to the new name only (D8); lines naming the Settings tabs keep "tab". Revisit never under this plan.
- A "compact" density for small screens: a true non-goal (practitioner, `/review-plan` 2026-10-09); the window gets a minimum size (D4).
- A status-bar state/version label: dropped at `/review-plan` — the rail cue and the Record page's `state_label` already show the state, Settings → Status shows the version, and a non-permanent status-bar widget hides while any message shows (every `showMessage` in this app has no timeout and `clearMessage` is never called). The status bar is styled only.
- A `theme.reset()` for tests: nothing needs a bare app once the conftest fixture makes the theme the rule.
- A new UI framework or new Python dependency (QML, qt-material, qdarkstyle, Fluent widget packs): excluded — PySide6 only (`docs/design-system.md` "No new UI framework"), the installer's PyInstaller spec bundles no extra assets today, and a third-party stylesheet would own values the design system must own.
- Rich text (HTML) in any label or text view to get emphasis: excluded — labels are `PlainText` by security choice (user-written text is never read as markup; tests pin it); emphasis comes from the stylesheet (D1).
- Collapsible groups, sub-tabs or stacked pages INSIDE a screen around tested widgets: excluded — `isVisibleTo` assertions and the Practitioner scroll test (C6).

### Accepted Assumptions — Revalidate Later
- Qt's Fusion style under a stylesheet and a `QPalette` renders acceptably on Windows 11 at 100 %, 125 % and 150 % scaling. Verified in planning: the offscreen test platform ALREADY uses Fusion (production uses `windows11`), so Phase 1 makes production match what every UI test has always rendered. Revalidate at the Phase 1 smoke (three scalings, light and dark).
- `QStyleHints.colorScheme()` reports the Windows app-mode setting and `colorSchemeChanged` fires when it changes (PySide6 6.11.1 in the venv; `pyproject.toml` pins `>=6.8`; on the real platform it returned `Dark` on this computer — Windows is in dark app mode here, so the dark variant is what the practitioner sees first). Revalidate at the Phase 1 smoke by flipping Settings → Personalisation → Colours while the app runs; if the signal does not fire on this machine, the fallback is a re-read at every window activation (D3).
- The window's title bar follows Windows app mode by Qt's default on this platform; if it does not at the Phase 1 smoke, the `windows:darkmode=2` platform argument is a follow-up (accepted, `/review-plan` 2026-10-09).
- PyInstaller bundles the five new modules without a spec change: `packaging/scribe.spec:48-51` enumerates `APP_MODULES` by `PACKAGE.rglob("*.py")` (pinned by `test_build_spec.py:187-188`); no icon file, no QtSvg (D12). Revalidate at Task 5.4.
- The install point: the weekend of 2026-10-10 → 11, after the last of today's four consultations is Completed or Discarded. If the build of record is not ready by then, the next window is any evening after the day's last Complete, before the next clinic day — never between consultations (D10).
- The clinic 1 smoke and the pilot are unaffected by the version change: the audit row records `app_version` per recording; the exit gate counts consultations "on a 0.2.0 or later build"; the smoke's drift replay runs "once per new build of record" (`plan-clinic-smoke.md:91`, its tallies row "Drift replays (one per build)"). Revalidate at P.2 (the replay on 0.4.0).
- `[2026-11-17, practitioner: "Accept, don't repeat (Recommended)"]` Task 5.4's local packaging smoke (PASS at `f5bb91b`, 2026-10-21) is NOT repeated after Phase M's merge: the merge adds no module, file asset or dependency (0.3.6 and 0.3.7 were each built of record by CI; this branch's five modules were bundled at `f5bb91b`). Revalidate at H5 (the CI build) and P.1 (the install checks).
- `[2026-11-17, practitioner: "Accept, no harness run (Recommended)"]` No validation-harness run on the merged build: outside `ui/` this branch changes six files (`__init__.py` version, `app.py` theme and taskbar calls, `audit.py` and `speaker_eval.py` display strings, `identity.py`, the new `taskbar.py`) and nothing in transcription or note generation; the merged suite (R12) and P.2's drift replay cover the rest. Revalidate at P.2.

### Key Design Decisions
- D1 — one theme module owns every value. D2 — Fusion + palette + one generated QSS, applied in `app.main()` and by one conftest fixture. D3 — the Windows scheme is followed live, owned by the theme module. D4 — the rail, header and Settings strip are widgets OUTSIDE the screens driving the existing `QTabWidget` with its bar hidden; the status bar is styled only. D5 — Settings is the gear plus the strip over pages 5–8; no wrapper page. D6 — the Review screen is a lite composition of today's two screens (both engines whole; `_adopt` = `addWidget`). D7 — the checklist (0.4.1) computes one report from existing seams, refreshed by navigation and a 5 s timer. D8 — wording: hierarchy first, ratified lines verbatim, renamed screens only. D9 — every privacy rule is kept by construction (Critical Constraints). D10 — the build and the install point. D11 — the Chrome panel later. D12 — painter-primitive icons. D13 — tests pin the theme once, never the rendering. D14 — the right pane is show/hide; `isVisibleTo` ancestors.

## Key Findings

### Files / Symbols Involved
- `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/app.py:747` creates the `QApplication` (an early refusal path at :723); `MainWindow(...)` at :819, shown :879. Nothing sets a style, palette or font today.
- `ui/main_window.py:453` `MainWindow(QMainWindow)`; `:495` title "Clinic Scribe"; `:738-751` `self.tabs = QTabWidget()` with nine `addTab`s (Microphone, Session, Recovery, Transcript, Note, Past sessions, Practitioner, Clinics, Status) and `currentChanged` → `_on_tab_changed` (:1058-1068: by widget IDENTITY it clears the Past sessions tab's decrypted text on leaving it and refreshes Recovery); `StatusPanel` :262; `self.note_screen.write_requested.connect(self._on_write_requested)` :805 (pinned literal). No window icon, no minimum size.
- Today's ad-hoc styling — the ONLY styling in the package — is 15 `setStyleSheet` calls on single widgets plus one `QFrame.StyledPanel` (16 sites): error red `#b00020` (×11: `ui/clinics.py:93`, `ui/microphone.py:134,149`, `ui/recovery.py:129,163`, `ui/transcript.py:191`, `ui/practitioner.py:458`, `ui/note.py:654,2142`), bold (×9: `ui/practitioner.py:396,415`, `ui/note.py:651,659,1026`, and the bold-red ones), the link-styled `change_role_button` (`ui/transcript.py:254-258`, `#0645ad`, flat, underlined, hand cursor); one `QFrame.StyledPanel` per proposal row (`ui/note.py:1023`). No `setObjectName` on any widget (`ui/note.py:346` copies a QAction's name); no `setSpacing`/`QSpacerItem`; `addStretch(1)` ends most vertical layouts and most button rows (not the Session row, `ui/session_screen.py:206-214`); `setContentsMargins(0,0,0,0)` only for nesting (`ui/past_sessions.py:279,328,340`, `ui/practitioner.py:685`).
- Every message label is a `QLabel` with `setTextFormat(PlainText)` + `setWordWrap(True)`; three private `_plain_label()` helpers (`ui/clinics.py:53`, `ui/past_sessions.py:154`, `ui/style_review.py:132`). No rich text anywhere in `ui/` (`setHtml`/`toHtml`/`<b>` absent). Text views are `QPlainTextEdit`.
- Lists and combos: `ui/lists.py:30` `NoCopyListWidget`, `:52` `NoCopyComboBox` (its `_DropCopy` filter on `view()` is why `setView` is banned).
- The copy surface: `ui/note.py:359-381` `_place_note_text(text, *, shadow)` (the one clipboard write, three Windows formats); `:384-443` `_NotePanel`; `:270-356` `_LineEditor` (standard-menu action object names `edit-copy`/`edit-cut` matched at :247-267); `:2363` `_copy_allowed`, `:2370` `_copy_ready`, `:2394-2417` `_apply_copy_binding` (the only place that makes `note_body` selectable); `:708` `self.write_button.clicked.connect(self._on_write_clicked)` (pinned literal).
- Screens (widget trees in the shell design pass): `ui/session_screen.py:56` (labels, two never-pre-ticked checkboxes, the five-button row, progress, message); `ui/transcript.py:102` (warning, live header, `transcript_view`, legend/attribution/link/shadow labels, the hidden "Generate note" group :240 with the role radios built at :512, profile and prefill combos, Generate, progress; Complete/Discard row; message); `ui/note.py:446` (a 1:1 `QSplitter` :764 — left: transcript label + view :585 + the "Edit the note" group :626 with its `QScrollArea` :614 round `_lines_box`; right: info/consent/blocking/review labels, `_NotePanel`, style label, `proposals_scroll` :745, the five-button row :758, shadow/write/message labels); `ui/past_sessions.py:161` (status, splitter: three group boxes left, the opened entry right); `ui/practitioner.py:272` (ten groups inside `scroll_area`); `ui/clinics.py:61` (the app's only `QFormLayout` :107); `ui/microphone.py:69`; `ui/recovery.py:63` (two bare header labels, two lists, exactly five push buttons).
- `ui/models.py`: ~104 user-visible string constants (`*_LINE`, `*_LABEL`, `*_MESSAGE`, `*_HEADER`, `*_TOOLTIP`…) and text builders returning plain `str`; `ui/past_sessions_view.py:48-665` ~80 more. `INTENDED_USE_LINE` :3132, `FIRST_RUN_BANNER` :3139 / `first_run_banner_text` :3150.
- Tests: no conftest `QApplication`; ~20 UI modules each do `os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM","offscreen")` and a module-scoped `QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])`, building `MainWindow` / screens directly (`test_ui_screens.py:109-114`, `_main_window()` :1052-1092). `app.main()` is run against the shared pytest app, with fake windows, by `test_audit.py:1702,1923`, `test_exclusions.py:1339`, `test_hands_free.py:644`, `test_frozen_runtime.py:416`, `test_status_and_app.py:689,732`; `test_frozen_runtime.py:259,387` make `QApplication` fail on some paths. Nothing in `tests/` asserts `styleSheet()`, `font()`, `palette()`, colours or `objectName()`.
- Packaging: `packaging/scribe.spec:53-56` `APP_DATAS` and `pyproject.toml:70-73` bundle only `config_defaults/*.json`; the spec strips Qt networking. A `.qss`/icon/font FILE needs both lists — so the theme is Python strings (D1) and the icons are drawn in code (D12); `APP_MODULES` (`scribe.spec:48-51`) globs every `.py`, so the new modules ship with no spec change.
- Version places (pinned equal by `test_install_layout.py:865-880`): `desktop/pyproject.toml:7`, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/__init__.py:3`, `extension/src/manifest.ts:29`, `extension/package.json:4`, `extension/package-lock.json` (two entries).
- Chrome panel (Phase 6): `extension/src/panel.html:7-115` holds all the CSS inline with `:root` variables and a dark block; `panel.ts` draws with `createElement` + `textContent`, `data-part`/`data-layout` attributes and one class `button.primary`; `page.ts` styles the frame and block card through the CSSOM only (never an inline `<style>` — Cliniko's CSP). `sinks.test.ts:47-70` scans `panel.html` for bare tokens including `write`, `storage`, `cookie`; `panel.dom.test.ts` never loads the CSS but pins `[data-part]`, `[data-layout]`, `button[data-action]`, the consent label's exact `textContent`, same-node buttons across timer ticks, `role="alert"`.

### Codebase Integration Notes
- The whole suite is offscreen Qt; a theme applied in `app.main()` is invisible to the screen tests, and one applied on the shared pytest app LEAKS into later tests. D2 makes the theme the suite's rule (one autouse fixture) and D13 says what tests pin. The offscreen platform's default style is already Fusion; production's is `windows11` — Phase 1 makes production match what every UI test has always rendered.
- Qt facts the executor must not rediscover (probed offscreen at `/review-plan`): `QLayout.removeWidget` on a top-level layout is NOT recursive (a silent no-op for a widget in a nested row) and `QSplitter.layout()` is `None` — `target_layout.addWidget(w)` is what moves a widget (it detaches recursively and reparents); `setProperty` alone does not restyle — unpolish/polish is required; `tabBar().hide()` keeps `setCurrentWidget`, `currentChanged`, `widget(i)` and `tabText(i)` working; a hidden widget stays hidden across adoption and its later `show()` makes it `isVisibleTo` the new ancestor; adoption must happen in `__init__` before the window is shown; `QTabWidget` still handles Ctrl+Tab with the bar hidden (cycling VISIBLE tabs — `setTabVisible(i, False)` removes a page from the cycle without changing `count()`).
- `transcript_screen` / `note_screen` are referenced only from `ui/main_window.py` (`is_busy` at :1179 and :1482; the routing sites); `ui/bridge.py`, `ui/recovery.py`, `app.py`, `hotkey.py`, `voice_commands.py` never touch them. `nativeEvent` matches by the top-level `winId()`; the taskbar flash is `QApplication.alert(self)`; nothing depends on the central widget being `self.tabs`. No production code reads `currentWidget()`, `currentIndex()` or `isVisible()`; no `showEvent`/`hideEvent` override exists in the package.
- `test_shadow_exits.py` counts constructions by `(file, "Class.method")` — moving a pinned construction into a helper, or a widget into another file, is a pin change even when nothing else changes. The Review merge (D6) is designed around this.
- Tests route by identity (`window.tabs.currentWidget() is window.<x>_screen`) and read ~200 widget attributes by name from `window.<screen>.<attr>`. The shell (D4) and the merge (D6) keep every screen object and attribute name; each phase's deliberate test changes are named in that phase (one title-list edit per page-list change, plus Phase 3's landing re-point — PR-MED-002).
- Every refusal line, tooltip, placeholder and button text the tests read is unchanged by this plan unless Phase 0's wording decision lists it; a listed relabel is a named test change.
- `docs/lessons.md`: nothing verified from an agent shell counts for Windows state; the live smokes are the practitioner's.

### External / API Findings
- Qt 6.11 (PySide6 6.11.1): `QGuiApplication.styleHints().colorScheme()` → `Qt.ColorScheme.{Unknown,Light,Dark}`, signal `colorSchemeChanged(Qt.ColorScheme)`; `QStyleFactory.create("Fusion")` is the conventional base for stylesheet theming (the default "windows11" style and a stylesheet fight over control painting). A stylesheet that sets `QPushButton` colours must also set the disabled/hover/pressed states or Fusion's defaults show through; `QComboBox QAbstractItemView` styles the popup without replacing the view (C3).

## Planned Workflow Summary

### Flow 1 — A clinic day on 0.4.0
- The app opens on Record (the header strip names the Chrome link state; the rail shows Record selected). The practitioner opens the patient's note in Chrome; the header shows the link line and, once a recording is live, the live line (the verified patient and clinic) — nothing else; the consent tick and Start are on one card. Start → the rail's Record entry shows REC; Pause/Resume/Finish as today. Finish → the app lands on Review: transcript left, the Generate group right; Generate → the note fills the right pane under the (now disabled) group; Save → Write draft → (reload in Chrome) → Complete, all on the one button row; the Record screen says what was kept.

### Flow 2 — First open on a fresh computer (0.4.0, then 0.4.1)
- 0.4.0: as today — with no voice profile the window opens on Settings → Practitioner with the one-line banner (`FIRST_RUN_BANNER`); recording works without it. 0.4.1: the window opens on the setup checklist with four rows (microphone, clinic key, voice profile, Chrome link), each with a "Set up" button to its Settings page; the rows refresh on every navigation and every 5 s while the page is shown; "Continue to Record" always works; when all four hold the page leaves the rail and the Ctrl+Tab cycle and is not shown again in that process.

### Flow 3 — Windows switches to dark
- The theme listens for the scheme change and re-applies the DARK tokens: palette, stylesheet and the rail's cue colours, without re-creating any widget; every label and text view keeps its text and interaction flags.

### Flow 4 — The weekend install
- After the last Complete of Friday 2026-10-09: close the app and Chrome; verify the 0.4.0 download (attestation + hash); run `setup.exe`; reload the extension; restart Chrome; open Clinic Scribe; Settings → Status names the computer's Chrome link and version 0.4.0; run the drift replay once for the new build; Monday's first consultation runs on 0.4.0 in shadow mode as the smoke requires — the smoke plan's daily line now reads "Settings → Status: Shadow mode on; Settings → Practitioner: writing style `clean`" (its dated addendum, Task 5.2).

## Design Decisions
- **D1 — One theme module owns every visual value.** New `ui/theme.py`: a frozen `Tokens` dataclass (colour roles: `canvas`, `surface`, `surface_alt`, `border`, `text`, `text_muted`, `accent`, `on_accent`, `danger`, `warning`, `success`, `focus`, `link`, `rec`, `paused`; type: `family`, `size_body`, `size_caption`, `size_title`, `size_heading`; spacing: `space_1..5`; `radius_control`, `radius_card`), `LIGHT` and `DARK` instances, `render_qss(tokens) -> str` producing ONE stylesheet whose selectors are widget classes plus dynamic properties (`QLabel[role="danger"]`, `QLabel[role="muted"]`, `QLabel[role="title"]`, `QPushButton[variant="primary"|"danger"|"link"]`, `QFrame[card="true"]`, and the shell's object ids `#rail #railButton #railCue #header #appTitle #settingsStrip #settingsTabs`), and `set_role(widget, role)` / `set_variant(button, variant)` / `mark_card(frame)`, which set a dynamic property and unpolish/polish the widget (Qt does not restyle on `setProperty` alone — probed). The 16 ad-hoc style sites (15 `setStyleSheet` calls and one `StyledPanel`) become those calls; `#b00020`, `#0645ad` and `font-weight: bold` disappear from every other module (a test greps for them — D13). Values live in code; `docs/design-system.md` gets a "Tokens" section naming the roles and pointing at `ui/theme.py` for the values. Rationale: the only way a stylesheet can be tested, packaged (strings, no new `APP_DATAS`) and kept from drifting. Alternatives rejected: a `.qss` file (packaging + no tests), a third-party theme (owns the values), rich text for emphasis (security choice, pinned).
- **D2 — Fusion + palette + stylesheet, applied in TWO places: `app.main()` and one conftest fixture.** `theme.apply_theme(app, scheme=None)` sets, in this order, `QStyleFactory.create("Fusion")` (once), a `QPalette` built from the tokens (so native-painted parts — scrollbars, menus, message boxes, file dialogs' chrome — match, including the disabled group and `PlaceholderText`), the application font (Segoe UI Variable Text → Segoe UI → Arial by `QFont.setFamilies`) and the stylesheet; it stamps `app.setProperty("scribe_scheme", …)` and emits a module-level `ThemeBus.changed(Tokens)`. `theme.ensure_applied(app)` is idempotent through an app property and connects `colorSchemeChanged` exactly once. Production: called in `app.main()` on the line after `app = QApplication([])` (`app.py:747`) and BEFORE `acquire_instance_exclusion()` (`:752`), because the two refusal boxes (`:362/:367`) run INSIDE the guard's refusal branches (`:753-760`, each of which returns) — a call after the guard could never theme them (codex round 1 PR-MED-001); the theme reads only the app's own `styleHints()` and sets its style, palette, font and stylesheet, so a refused second instance still constructs no controller and touches no sweep or store (the guard's invariant is about shared data, not about Qt); never on the `:723` not-installed path, never creating an app (`test_frozen_runtime.py:259,387`). Tests: a function-scoped autouse fixture `_themed_qapp` in `desktop/tests/conftest.py` calls `ensure_applied` whenever a `QApplication` instance exists — pytest sets up the modules' module-scoped `qapp` fixtures first, so every UI test whose app comes from such a fixture, standalone screens included, renders under the theme deterministically; the FOUR tests that create the app inside their own body — `test_status_and_app.py:55` (`test_window_offscreen_smoke`, builds `MainWindow`), `test_install_layout.py:850` (builds `StatusPanel`), `test_shadow_exits.py:582, 610` (the list and combo copy tests) — run after the fixture has already found no instance, so each gains one `theme.ensure_applied(app)` line after its creation (Task 1.2; codex round 3 PR-LOW-006 — verified by a sweep of every in-body `QApplication(` site in `tests/`, the other 21 being module fixtures) (the geometry pins in `test_ui_writing_style.py:124-129` all assert "taller than / at least", and the sheet only adds height). The `main()`-driven tests (`test_audit.py:1702,1923`, `test_exclusions.py:1339`, `test_hands_free.py:644`, `test_status_and_app.py:689,732`, `test_frozen_runtime.py:416`) patch `QApplication` to a REAL shared instance, so `setStyle`/`setPalette` exist and their fake windows receive no call. No call in `MainWindow.__init__` (redundant in both production and tests; it would add a `theme` → `QApplication` dependency to `main_window.py` for nothing) and no `theme.reset` (nothing needs a bare app). `ui/note.py` never touches `QApplication` beyond `.clipboard()` (C9). Rationale (simplicity lens, verified): the pre-window boxes need the `main()` call; standalone-screen tests need the fixture; a third site adds only an ordering question.
- **D3 — The Windows scheme is followed live, owned by `ui/theme.py`.** `theme.detect_scheme(app)`: `Qt.ColorScheme.Dark` → "dark"; `Light`, `Unknown` (the offscreen platform) or a missing attribute → "light". `ensure_applied` connects `app.styleHints().colorSchemeChanged` once to re-run `apply_theme` (palette + stylesheet → Qt repolishes every widget; nothing is re-created; the rail re-tints its icons on `ThemeBus.changed`). The test seam is `theme.force_scheme("dark" | "light" | None)` (module state read by `apply_theme` when `scheme` is None; a test flips it inside a `try/finally`) — never `QStyleHints.setColorScheme`, which would change the platform's state for the whole process; so DARK is testable only through the seam. No window injection. Fallback if the signal proves inert at the Phase 1 smoke: re-read on `QEvent.ApplicationActivate`.
- **D4 — The shell is a rail, a header strip and a Settings strip OUTSIDE the screens, driving the existing `QTabWidget` with its tab bar hidden; the status bar is styled only.** `self.tabs` stays a `QTabWidget` (`setDocumentMode(True)`, `tabBar().hide()`) so every `count()` / `tabText()` / `currentWidget()` / `setCurrentWidget()` / `currentChanged` use in `main_window.py` (15 `setCurrentWidget` sites — `:785, 1177, 1181, 1188, 1195, 1202, 1233, 1281, 1293, 1507, 1546, 1659, 2019, 2046, 2112` — and `widget(index)` at :1059) and in the tests (25 sites across four files) keeps working, and `_on_tab_changed`'s identity rule (Past sessions clears on leave, Recovery refreshes on entry) is untouched — ONE call, `self._sync_shell(widget)`, is appended to it (the rail and the strip each expose `set_current(widget)` and block their own signals inside it). Page order per phase (each phase's title-list edit of `test_ui_screens.py:3515-3530` is its navigation test change; Phase 3 also re-points the profile-present landing assertion `:4167` to Record, PR-MED-002): after Phase 2 (Review) — eight: Microphone, Session, Recovery, Review, Past sessions, Practitioner, Clinics, Status; after Phase 3 (shell) — eight, reordered and renamed: 0 "Record" (`session_screen`) · 1 "Review" (`review_screen`) · 2 "Past sessions" · 3 "Recovery" · 4 "Microphone" · 5 "Practitioner" · 6 "Clinics" · 7 "Status" (4–7 = `SETTINGS_PAGES`); after 0.4.1's Task 6.3 — nine with "Set up" (`setup_page`) at 0. New `ui/shell.py`: `NavigationRail(QFrame#rail)` — checkable `QToolButton#railButton`s with `setAutoExclusive(False)` and manual exclusivity in `set_current` (a page with NO entry — Settings pages, the 0.4.1 Set up page after completion — shows NO lit entry; an exclusive group cannot uncheck every button), text under icon: "Record", "Review", "Past sessions", "Recovery", stretch, "Settings" (gear), `page_requested = Signal(QWidget)`, and a `QLabel#railCue` under Record reading "REC" / "PAUSED" (`models.rail_cue_text(state)`) from a new `SessionScreen.state_shown = Signal(object)` emitted in `refresh()` (:286) when the state differs from a NEW `_last_shown` field (never `_last_state`, which `_watch_state` :269-284 reads for the FAILED transition) — the existing 500 ms state watch, no new source (C8); `HeaderStrip(QFrame#header)` — "Clinic Scribe" (role title) and ONE elided `PlainText` label fed by a new `SessionScreen.chrome_view_changed = Signal(str)` emitted in `set_chrome_view` (:459-462; the bridge calls it on the GUI thread, `ui/bridge.py:1391`) — its text is built STRUCTURALLY from the `ChromeView`: `models.header_link_text(view)` = the link line (`CHROME_WAITING_LINE` / `CHROME_CONNECTED_LINE` / `CHROME_UNAVAILABLE_LINE`) plus the live line ONLY when `view.clinic is not None` (`chrome_view_text` :1106-1135 puts a hotkey, recheck, block or refusal line second otherwise — never in the header); empty and hidden with no bridge, so every test window has none; while the Past sessions page is current the live line is withheld (`_sync_shell` passes the page) so "Hide names" keeps its meaning there (practitioner, `/review-plan`); `SettingsStrip(QFrame#settingsStrip)` — a caption "Settings" (role muted) and a `QTabBar#settingsTabs` (not an item view; outside C2) with the four settings titles read from `tabs.tabText(i)`, shown only while a settings page is current. The status bar: `QMainWindow.statusBar()` unchanged (its `currentMessage()` pins hold; every `showMessage` here has no timeout and nothing calls `clearMessage`, so a non-permanent widget there would vanish at the first pause message — the state/version labels were dropped at `/review-plan`); `QStatusBar {}` styled by the sheet. `MainWindow.show_page(widget)` walks up to the direct page and calls `setCurrentWidget`. Keyboard: `QTabWidget` still cycles VISIBLE tabs on Ctrl+Tab with the bar hidden — accepted for the settings pages (the strip syncs); the 0.4.1 Set up page leaves the cycle through `setTabVisible(i, False)` at completion (keeps `count()` and `tabText`). Window: `setWindowIcon(icons.app_icon())`, `setMinimumSize(960, 640)`, `resize(1200, 800)` (no test pins geometry). Rationale: least code, the 25-site test surface and the identity rule survive untouched, "no new UI framework". Alternatives rejected: `QStackedWidget` (loses `tabText`, no gain); a nested Settings stack behind a facade (hidden-ancestor trap for `isVisibleTo`, most code); a West `QTabBar` as the rail (rotated text — a readable icon-over-text rail would need a painting subclass; the REC cue would have to live in `tabText`).
- **D5 — Settings is the gear plus the Settings strip over pages 4–7 of `self.tabs`; no wrapper page, no sub-list.** The four setup screens stay direct pages of the one container (so `window.tabs.currentWidget() is window.practitioner_screen` at `test_ui_screens.py:4128` holds; `:4167`'s `is window.microphone_screen` holds through Phase 2 only — Phase 3 makes Record page 0 with the landing rule untouched, so a profile-present window opens on Record by the container's default and Task 3.5 re-points that assertion to `window.session_screen` (codex round 1 PR-MED-002); every `isVisibleTo(screen)` relationship is untouched and the Practitioner `QScrollArea` test holds — C6/C7); the rail's gear opens the first of them (Microphone) and the strip switches between them. The intended-use line and the start-up exclusion warnings keep heading the Status page and the Past sessions page (design-system rule kept). Opening Settings is a rail entry; leaving it is the rail again (no modal, no Close button to pin). The earlier "hosted inside a `SettingsView` with a left sub-list" wording was struck at `/review-plan` — it contradicted D4 and would have broken the identity tests.
- **D6 — The Review screen is a LITE COMPOSITION of today's two screens: both engines kept whole, two adoptions and one hide.** New `ui/review.py` `ReviewScreen(QWidget)` takes the existing `TranscriptScreen` and `NoteScreen` instances (built unchanged at `ui/main_window.py:582-610`, so the pinned `write_requested` connect at :805 and the `EncounterRecord` count stay) as its CHILDREN — visible, not hidden — in a horizontal `QSplitter`: LEFT = the Transcript engine whole (once `generate_box`, its button row and its `message_label` are taken out, its own layout `ui/transcript.py:316-327` is already "warning, live header, transcript_view, legend, attribution, link, shadow" top to bottom); RIGHT = a `QVBoxLayout` of `generate_box` (adopted out of the Transcript engine — its show/hide logic at `:294, :476, :691` untouched) over the Note engine whole with its `transcript_panel` hidden by ONE `hide()` (so `note_side` fills its own splitter; `NoteScreen.__init__` exposes `self.transcript_pane = transcript_panel` — a pin-neutral attribute line) over `edit_group` (adopted out of `transcript_panel`'s layout, `ui/note.py:634`, so "Edit the note" sits under the note); then ONE button row `save_button · write_button · copy_button · stretch · complete_button · discard_button`; then a status strip of the two existing `message_label`s (Transcript's custody/generation lines above Note's note-scoped lines), `write_label` and the note `shadow_label`. The ONE mechanism is `_adopt(widget, layout, *args)` = `layout.addWidget(widget, *args)` — Qt's `addChildWidget` detaches the widget from its old layout RECURSIVELY and reparents it; `QLayout.removeWidget` on a top-level layout is a silent no-op for a widget in a nested row and `QSplitter.layout()` is `None` (probed) — build the target layouts parented (`QVBoxLayout(pane)`) and adopt only inside `ReviewScreen.__init__`, before the window is shown; never re-adopt at run time. Every widget keeps its object, owner, signals and enablement logic (`TranscriptScreen._update_controls` still drives Complete — tooltip from `complete_block_reason` — and the one-click Discard on a stopped session; `NoteScreen._apply_copy_binding` / `_apply_write_binding` still drive Copy, `note_body` and Write; the engines' `_clear_layout` helpers touch only sub-layouts that travel inside adopted or untouched widgets — `_lines_box` inside `edit_group`, `_role_box` inside `generate_box`, `_blocking_box` / `_review_box` / `_proposals_box` inside `note_side`, which is never split). `_place_note_text`, `_LineEditor`, `_build_editor_row`, `_lines_box`, `_NotePanel` and `_apply_copy_binding` are untouched; `ReviewScreen` constructs no text widget, list, combo, clipboard, drag or `QApplication` call (C1, C2, C9 unchanged); re-parenting changes no text-interaction flag and the Copy/Cut interception lives in the widgets' own `keyPressEvent`/`contextMenuEvent` (no `QShortcut` or event filter on either engine). Tests keep reading `window.transcript_screen.<attr>` and `window.note_screen.<attr>`; the Review page is `window.review_screen`; because the left-pane widgets stay INSIDE the Transcript engine, every `isVisibleTo(window.transcript_screen)` assertion on `live_header_label` / `warning_label` stays truthful — only `generate_box`'s one (`test_unreviewed_review.py:613`) moves to `review_screen` (D14). Rationale: meets every element of the decision (transcript left, note right, Generate on the right before a note exists, Edit the note under the note, one button row) with ZERO shadow-exit pin changes, no name collisions (`message_label`, `shadow_label`, `transcript_view`, `is_busy`, `clear` exist on both), both engines constructible standalone for their ~30 standalone tests, and ten fewer test rewrites than the full composition. The full composition (every left-pane widget adopted into ReviewScreen's own pane, the engines hidden, NoteScreen's duplicate `QPlainTextEdit` at `:584-594` and its splitter removed — the ONE shadow-exit pin change at `test_shadow_exits.py:109`) is 0.4.1's Task 6.4. Alternatives rejected: absorbing `transcript.py` into `note.py` (the custody engine inside the pinned file; collisions); keeping two tabs with Complete added to Note (does not meet the decision). Microcopy that names the old screens is Task 4.1 (D8).
- **D7 — The setup checklist (0.4.1) is ONE report computed from existing seams and state, refreshed by navigation and a timer — no new file, no per-seam hooks.** New `ui/setup_checklist.py` `SetupChecklistPage(QWidget)`: one card, title "Set up Clinic Scribe", four rows (tick label "✓" role success / "○" role muted, a title, a `PlainText` detail line, a "Set up" `QPushButton` emitting `page_requested(<settings page>)`), then "Continue to Record" (always enabled — it never blocks) and a muted line reusing `FIRST_RUN_BANNER`'s promise that recording works without a voice profile. The Qt-free decision lives in `ui/models.py`: `SetupItem(name, done, detail)`, `setup_checklist(microphone, clinic, profile, chrome) -> tuple[SetupItem, ...]`, `setup_complete(items)`. `MainWindow._setup_items()` reads the four facts: microphone — `self.microphone_screen.selected_device_id() is not None` (`ui/microphone.py:222-224`); clinic key — `self._clinic_registry.records` non-empty and `.load_problem is None` (`scribe_desktop/clinics.py:497,505`; the window already holds the registry at `main_window.py:646-648` — never reach into the screen); voice profile — `self.practitioner_screen.profile_present` (`ui/practitioner.py:867-869`) with the readiness reason as the detail; Chrome link — `StatusPanel.refresh_registration()` (`main_window.py:440-445`) keeps `self.registration_status` (initialised in `StatusPanel.__init__` to "not checked"), the tick is `.registered` — a registry read through the injected layer, never a Cliniko call, never a model load. `setup_page.refresh(items)` runs (a) at the end of `MainWindow.__init__` AFTER the rail exists, (b) from `_on_tab_changed` (every navigation — the practitioner always returns from a Settings page through the rail), (c) from one 5 s `QTimer` started in the page's `showEvent` and stopped in `hideEvent` (the `ui/microphone.py:182-183` pattern) — a tick lags at most 5 s. Detail lines reuse existing text (`first_run_banner_text`, `registration_lines(status)[0]`, the device name). Landing rule (replaces `ui/main_window.py:781-785`): incomplete → `setCurrentWidget(setup_page)`, the rail shows "Set up"; complete → Record. `completed = Signal()` (connected after the first refresh) when a refresh finds all four done: the rail entry hides, `tabs.setTabVisible(i, False)` removes the page from the Ctrl+Tab cycle (keeps `count()` and `tabText`), and if the page is current the window moves to Record; it does not return in that process. "One-time" is therefore computed — a fresh machine always gets it, a set-up machine never does, and no `config\` file is added (any new settings write would also change the smoke's config hashes mid-run). `PractitionerScreen.show_first_run_banner`, `profile_changed` (already emitted at `:865`) and `FIRST_RUN_BANNER` stay (their screen-level tests `test_ui_screens.py:1210-1235`, `test_ui_learn_style.py:541-559`, `test_ui_models.py:1538-1541` keep passing); only the window stops calling the banner — the two window-level tests (`test_ui_screens.py:4121-4134`, `:4137-4176`) become the checklist's (Task 6.3). Every tick in a test is INJECTED both ways (C14).
- **D8 — Wording: hierarchy first; ratified lines verbatim; renamed screens only.** The redesign changes how text is RANKED (title / body / muted / danger roles, cards, group spacing), not what the sentences say. Kept verbatim: every consent text (`CONSENT_TEXT_V3`, `RECORDING_CONSENT_LABEL`, `DEVELOPMENT_CONSENT_LABEL`), `INTENDED_USE_LINE`, `RETENTION_WARNING`, every `WRITE_LINES` entry, every tooltip and placeholder a test reads, every two-click label, and every line that names a Settings tab ("on the Clinics tab", "the Status tab", "the Practitioner tab", "the Microphone tab" — `ui/models.py:286, 295, 299, 348, 391, 970, 2724, 2728, 2735, 2893, 2995, 3017, 3195, 3644, 3666, 3670, 3678`, `ui/session_screen.py:545`, `ui/style_review.py:77` and their pins — NOT `ui/practitioner.py:505`, which sits on a Settings page but names the Note tab, codex round 1 PR-LOW-003) because the Settings strip's `QTabBar` carries exactly those four titles, so the words stay literally true (practitioner, `/review-plan` 2026-10-09). What CHANGES (Task 4.1, each with its pin named): every line naming a RENAMED screen — "Session tab" (`ui/models.py:890, 915, 995`; `extension/src/panel-view.ts:39` → "Record page", the one Chrome string, with its `panel-view.test.ts` pin), "Note tab" / "Transcript screen" / "this tab" (`ui/transcript.py:857`; `ui/note.py:692, 1678, 2192, 2317`; `ui/note_review.py:286, 344, 356, 428`; `ui/models.py:969, 1002, 1029, 1533, 2086, 2739, 2771`; `ui/practitioner.py:505` "How the note body reads on the Note tab" → "on the Review screen", no test pin (PR-LOW-003); `speaker_eval.py:1229` — the measurement report's "what the Transcript screen pre-checks" → "what the Review screen pre-checks", emitted report text, no test pin (codex round 2 PR-LOW-005; code comments and docstrings that name the old screens are not user-visible and stay); `unlearned_on_exit_line`, pinned `test_ui_screens.py:8499`), "Recovery tab" → "Recovery page" (`ui/models.py:1005`; `ui/session_screen.py:644, 818`), "Past sessions tab" (`audit.py:182, 185`; `ui/main_window.py:246` → "Past sessions page"); pins beyond :8499 to re-point: `test_ui_models.py:382, 680, 697, 1097`, `test_ui_learn_style.py:928`, `test_write_lines.py:75, 110, 253`, `test_note_review.py:459, 479, 528`, `test_save_as_ratification.py:481`, `test_ui_screens.py:2218`, `test_audit.py:304`. A rewritten line that promises a deferred outcome still names the CONTROL and the SCREEN ("press Save note on the Review screen" — `docs/lessons.md` 2026-09-17). Group titles, button labels and help-line-to-tooltip moves are NOT in this plan (0.2 was narrowed at `/review-plan` to the renamed-screen list above). No line is shortened by removing a remedy, a law or a limit; the Chrome reason CODES never change.
- **D9 — Every privacy rule is kept by construction** (the Critical Constraints are the pin inventory; each phase lists the pins it touches and the ONE named test change it makes).
- **D10 — Version 0.4.0, built in the developer channel, installed at a weekend install point.** The practitioner's timing: "4 patients left for today and then it's the weekend". The install point is after the day's last Complete/Discard and before the next clinic day — never between consultations; `docs/release/pilot-builds.md`'s verify-before-install and the pilot's rollback rule apply (rolling back 0.4.0 → 0.3.0 changes no store or schema — this plan adds no record field — so only "finish or discard every recording first" applies, not the "below 0.3.0" two steps). The smoke's tallies gain their per-build drift replay; the exit gate counts on. If the practitioner wants a clinic day on 0.4.0 before clinic 2's install, nothing here changes. `[2026-10-12 reconciliation, invented-text guard plan]` Superseded for any build that includes 0.3.2 (`.cursor/plans/plan-invented-text-guard.md`, which lands before this plan): 0.3.2 adds a stored transcript field (`suspect`, written only when true) and two note review codes, so rolling back below 0.3.2 follows `docs/release/pilot-builds.md`'s "Rolling back below 0.3.2" (most notes saved on 0.3.2 are unreadable below it; never Delete now on such an entry), not "only finish or discard every recording first". This plan's own changes still add no record field. `[2026-10-13 reconciliation, validation-pass plan]` And for any build that includes 0.3.3 (`.cursor/plans/plan-validation-pass.md`): two more stored review codes (`possible_next_patient`, `hedged_source`) and the session file `no-audio-kept`, which a build older than 0.3.3 ignores — so rolling back below 0.3.3 also follows `docs/release/pilot-builds.md`'s "Rolling back below 0.3.3" (Complete or Discard every recording lines were removed from first). The Review screen adopts that plan's codes, the chooser's `[after possible next patient]` mark and the Transcript tab's "Remove lines N-end from this recording..." (its line, button, confirmation and refusal copy, `ui/models.py` `REMOVE_TAIL_*`) as data.
- **D11 — The Chrome panel is matched later** (Task 6.5, optional, after P.4): token VALUES only, into the existing `:root` variables; nothing in `panel.ts` / `panel-view.ts` / `page.ts` changes then (the one text change, `panel-view.ts:39`, is 0.4.0's Task 4.1); `sinks.test.ts:56-70` skips only lines starting with `//`, so no `/* */` comment or property value in `panel.html` may contain `write`, `writeln`, `storage`, `cookie`, `eval`, `Function` as a bare word or `.constructor`. The installed extension lives in `C:\Program Files\ClinikoScribe\extension`, so an extension-only change still ships through a build of record and an install.
- **D12 — Icons are painter primitives drawn in code, tinted by the theme; never an icon-only button.** New `ui/icons.py`: five shapes (record = ring + dot, review = document with lines, past sessions = clock, recovery = circular arrow, settings = gear) plus the app mark, drawn with `QPainterPath` on a `QPixmap` at 1× and 2× (`setDevicePixelRatio`) through `QPainter(pixmap)` — constructor form, then `painter.end()`; C1 bans any call named `begin(`; pen/brush from the tokens (`text_muted` normal, `accent` checked). No `QtSvg` import, no file asset, no PyInstaller branch (practitioner, `/review-plan` 2026-10-09; Fusion's `QStyle.StandardPixmap` icons were rejected — fixed-colour bitmaps that do not invert in dark and have no record or gear). The rail re-tints on `ThemeBus.changed`; every rail entry keeps its text; no screen button gains an icon in this plan (C5: tests read button texts). A test renders each name at both scales offscreen and checks a non-empty, non-uniform pixmap. Inline SVG through `QtSvg` is a deferred refinement.
- **D13 — Tests pin the theme once, never the rendering.** `tests/test_ui_theme.py`: the token sets are complete (every role present in both), light/dark differ where they must and agree where they must (danger stays red-family), `render_qss` mentions every role, variant and object id the code uses (a grep of `set_role(` / `set_variant(` / `mark_card(` / `setObjectName(` calls against the stylesheet) and the `QListWidget` min-height, no `#rrggbb`, `font-weight` or `setStyleSheet(` survives outside `ui/theme.py` (a package grep like `test_display_name.py`'s), `apply_theme` sets Fusion, the family and the scheme property, `ensure_applied` is idempotent (property set once, one connection), `set_role` is readable after the repolish, and `force_scheme("dark")` flips the palette's `Window` colour inside a `try/finally` that restores `None`. No pixel or screenshot test.
- **D14 — The right pane is show/hide, never a stack.** `generate_box` stays visible after Save so "Regenerate (replaces the saved note)" is reachable beside the saved note, and a disabled-with-reason control is shown, never hidden (design-system rule); the existing `show_document`'s `generate_box.setVisible(self._can_generate)` (:476) and `_clear`'s hide (:691) are the only visibility logic. A Qt fact the executor must know: `isVisibleTo(ancestor)` on a never-shown test window walks to the top-level and is False once a widget's ancestor is no longer the engine — so an `isVisibleTo(window.transcript_screen)` assertion on an ADOPTED widget must become `isVisibleTo(window.review_screen)` (a passing old assertion after adoption is a false negative). Under the lite composition (D6) only `generate_box` is adopted out of the Transcript engine, so exactly ONE site moves (`test_unreviewed_review.py:613`); the other ten (`test_ui_screens.py:3563, 3565, 3567, 3585, 3590, 3662, 4114-4116`; `test_unreviewed_review.py:667, 1014` — all `live_header_label` / `warning_label`, which stay inside the engine) move only with 0.4.1's Task 6.4. The state table (no session / recording / queued-no-note / generating / draft under review / saved / written / recovered / shadow) is Task 2.3's and is driven by the existing enablement code — `models.ControlSet` gains nothing (Complete/Discard depend on the lease and ratification, which the Session map cannot express; only the comment at `models.py:1378` changes).

## Schema / Data Changes
- None. No encrypted store, record, audit row or settings file changes (the checklist is computed from state, D7). The retention schedule and the threat model's data inventory are unchanged; only their screen NAMES are updated (Phase 5).

## Config / Environment / Deployment Impact
- Version 0.4.0 in the five places (`test_install_layout.py:865-880`); `packaging/scribe.spec` unchanged (no file asset, no QtSvg — D12); the installer and the model pack unchanged (no model change → the pack is not needed at install); an install never touches `%LOCALAPPDATA%` (`scribe.iss:68-82`), so the clinic smoke's config hashes (its D15) are unaffected.
- Release: `Release` workflow on `main`, attested, row in `docs/release/pilot-builds.md`; the extension is rebuilt (its version string changes) → reload + full Chrome restart at install.

## Critical Constraints
1. **C1 — The shadow-exit pins** (`desktop/tests/test_shadow_exits.py`, AST counts by `(file, "Class.method")`, exact): `_place_note_text` called exactly once each in `ui/note.py` `_NotePanel.copy_selection`, `NoteScreen._copy_note`, `_LineEditor._place_selection`, `ui/past_sessions.py` `PastSessionsScreen.on_copy`, keyword `shadow=` only; text widgets in `ui/note.py` and `ui/past_sessions.py` ONLY where counted today — `NoteScreen.__init__` 1 `QPlainTextEdit` + 1 `_NotePanel`, `NoteScreen._build_editor_row` 1 `_LineEditor`, `past_sessions._display_panel` 1 `QPlainTextEdit`; no other `QTextEdit`/`QTextBrowser`/`QLineEdit` or subclass in those two files; selectable-text flags only in `NoteScreen._apply_copy_binding`; `clipboard()`/`QMimeData`/`setMimeData`/`QDrag` only in `_place_note_text`; `setDragEnabled` exactly once (`_LineEditor.__init__`, literal `False`); `_LineEditor(..., shadow=)`; one Start call with `consent=` in `ui/session_screen.py`, one `EncounterRecord` in `ui/main_window.py`; every call named `begin(` passes `development_consent_version` — so **no `QPainter.begin`** (use `QPainter(self)` / `with`); no string literal exactly `"wave"`, `"read_recording"`, `"write_wav"`, `"wav_bytes"`, `"_recording_chunks"`, `"_audio_chunks"` anywhere (not as an object name, icon key or property value); `NoteScreen.__init__`, `NoteScreen._copy_note`, `PastSessionsScreen.on_copy`, `PastSessionsScreen.on_export_recording` defined directly in their class bodies. A phase that must move a pinned construction names the pin change as its ONE test change; no phase adds a text widget to those two files.
2. **C2 — Lists and combos**: every list is `ui/lists.NoCopyListWidget`, every combo `NoCopyComboBox`; no `QListView`/`QTreeView`/`QTableView`/`QColumnView`/`QFontComboBox`, no subclass of them, no `setView(`; popups styled only through QSS (`QComboBox QAbstractItemView {…}`). The rail is tool buttons and the Settings strip a `QTabBar` — neither is an item view.
3. **C3 — The clipboard menus**: `_LineEditor`'s standard-menu action object names (`edit-copy`, `edit-cut`) and English texts untouched; no `QTranslator`; `_NotePanel`'s menu texts "&Copy\t…" / "Select &All" kept; `createStandardContextMenu` kept.
4. **C4 — The pinned literals**: `self.write_button.clicked.connect(self._on_write_clicked)` inside `NoteScreen.__init__` as the only use; `self.note_screen.write_requested.connect(self._on_write_requested)` inside `MainWindow.__init__` as the only use; `QMainWindow.statusBar()` with `showMessage`/`currentMessage` kept and unchanged (styled by the sheet; no widget added to it — every message here has no timeout); window title "Clinic Scribe"; no "Cliniko Scribe"; no `ClinikoScribe` literal or "run scripts/…" text outside `install_layout.py`; no `getLogger`/`addHandler`/`basicConfig`/`qInstallMessageHandler` outside `logging_setup.py`.
5. **C5 — Names and texts the tests read stay**: every `window.<screen>` attribute and every widget attribute the tests read (`message_label`, `consent_checkbox`, `transcript_view`, `note_body`, `copy_button`, `write_button`, `save_button`, `complete_button`, `discard_button`, `generate_box`, `entry_list`, `retention_combo`, `scroll_area`, `scroll_content`, `_lines_box`, `_role_buttons`, `_proposal_buttons`, `_state_labels`, `_line_widgets`, …) keeps its name, class, text, tooltip and placeholder; `RecoveryScreen` has exactly its five `QPushButton`s; editor rows' "Edit"/"Apply"/"Cancel" are `QPushButton` direct children of the row; `_lines_box` holds only rows in order; `note_body` is a `_NotePanel`; labels the tests check stay `PlainText` (ALL message labels stay `PlainText` — emphasis via `set_role`).
6. **C6 — Visibility relationships**: every tested widget stays a descendant of its screen with no hidden ancestor between (no collapsible sections, sub-tabs or non-current stacked pages INSIDE a screen); the Practitioner screen keeps `scroll_area` (resizable, no horizontal bar) with `scroll_content` as its widget, and at 1200×400 the content overflows while `learned_rules_list.height() >= 40` — the stylesheet's paddings are checked against this test.
7. **C7 — Identity routing**: each screen object stays a DIRECT page of the ONE container the tests call `window.tabs` (`count()`, `tabText()`, `currentWidget()`, `setCurrentWidget()`, `currentChanged`) — the four setup screens included (no wrapper page, D5) — and `_on_tab_changed`'s identity rule — Past sessions drops its decrypted text and names on leave; Recovery refreshes on entry — keeps working through every navigation (rail, strip, `show_page`, the 0.4.1 `completed` move: all go through `setCurrentWidget`); the tab-list test (`test_ui_screens.py:3515-3530`) is the navigation test change of each phase that changes the page list (D4 names the three lists), with Phase 3's landing re-point (`:4167` → Record) the one addition.
8. **C8 — No new content path**: the header strip shows only the Chrome link line and, when a clinic is set on the live view, the live line — built structurally from the `ChromeView`, never by splitting the composed text (a hotkey, recheck, block or refusal line never reaches the header); withheld while Past sessions is current; the rail's REC/PAUSED cue derives from the session state machine; the 0.4.1 checklist reads only existing readiness seams; no label anywhere shows an id, a path or exception text (design-system Microcopy); the transcript stays `NoTextInteraction` everywhere it is shown; no new settings file (the smoke's config hashes, D15 of `plan-clinic-smoke.md`).
9. **C9 — `ui/note.py` uses `QApplication` for `.clipboard()` only** (tests stub it); the theme is applied from `app.py` and the conftest fixture, never from `note.py` or `main_window.py`.
10. **C10 — No new dependency, no file asset, no `QtSvg`**: PySide6 only; the stylesheet is a Python string; icons are painter primitives; a file asset (none planned) would go into BOTH `packaging/scribe.spec` `APP_DATAS` and `pyproject.toml` package-data in the same task, with `scripts/check-installed-sockets.py`'s offline check unaffected.
11. **C11 — Wording** (D8): no ratified, legal, custody or write line changes and no refusal line loses its substance or remedy; only lines naming a RENAMED screen change, each a named test change; the Chrome reason CODES never change; a rewritten deferred-outcome line names the control and the screen.
12. **C12 — The build and the install** (D10): built in the developer channel; the suite, ruff and mypy green; a build of record before any install; the install point after the day's last Complete/Discard; the pilot's rollback rule plus "reload the extension and restart Chrome" (an install replaces `{app}\extension` whole; `packaging/scribe.iss:68-82` writes `{app}` and HKLM only, never `%LOCALAPPDATA%`); the agent never installs, launches or verifies on this machine (`docs/lessons.md`).
13. **C13 — The theme never changes a control's enabled state, text interaction, echo mode, focus policy or tab order**; disabled controls stay visibly disabled (a disabled-state rule in the stylesheet for every control class), the API-key field stays `Password` echo, every two-click confirmation label still reads as the design system records, and `QMenu`, `QMessageBox`, `QDialog` and the combo popup are readable in both schemes.
14. **C14 — Every host-state input a test touches is injected both ways** (`docs/lessons.md` 2026-09-24): the scheme through `force_scheme`, the 0.4.1 checklist's four ticks through the window's seams (a fake `WindowsLayer`, a temporary registry, the fake backend's device, a profile root), each asserted "ticked" with the value injected `True` and "unticked" with `False` — never by what exists on the developer's machine (the conftest already pins the models root and makes the real `WindowsLayer` raise).
15. **C15 — Adoption happens once, in `ReviewScreen.__init__`, before the window is shown, through `addWidget` only**; `note_side` is never split (its warning rows are bare sub-layouts); no `removeWidget` / `setParent(None)` branch; no run-time re-adoption.

## Reconciliation — 2026-11-17 (against 0.3.4–0.3.7 as built)
Written at `/review-plan` on 2026-11-17 (planned on Fable 5.2 in Plan Mode; the practitioner's choice that day: "UI refresh merge"). `main` is at `0ae367d` (0.3.7, installed 2026-11-17, shadow mode on; workflow v49.0), 137 commits past the fork point `b08a920`; this branch is at `f93e047`, 10 commits past it and idle since 2026-10-21. Every fact below was probed on 2026-11-17 against an in-memory trial merge (`git merge-tree --write-tree claude/ui-refresh-bf9282 main`, tree `aa4f86b`), extracted to a scratch folder outside both checkouts; neither checkout was changed. Phase M's tasks carry the work; a task line affected by this section points here.

**R10 — The textual conflicts: 15 files, 22 regions. Each resolution is fixed here (Task M.1).** "Ours" is this branch, "theirs" is `main`.
- **Six version fields in five files** (`desktop/pyproject.toml`, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/__init__.py`, `extension/package.json`, `extension/package-lock.json` ×2, `extension/src/manifest.ts`): keep ours, `0.4.0`.
- **`desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/note.py`**, one region at the end of `NoteScreen.begin_review`: keep BOTH lines, theirs first — `self._begin_suggestions()` with its comment, then `self.review_changed.emit()` (the suggestion call returns early on its own guards, so the emit is always reached).
- **`desktop/tests/conftest.py`**, one region in the `from scribe_desktop import (...)` list: keep both names, alphabetical (`taskbar`, then `transcription`).
- **`desktop/tests/test_ui_models.py`**, one region at the end of the file: both sides appended; keep ours (`TestHeaderAndRailCue`) then theirs (the section-suggestion helpers and three classes).

  - THE CAUSE (S.9's line holds the full record): two widgets, one on each side. The transcript pane's legend was a non-wrapping line, holding that side at about 750 px. S.2's plain `QScrollArea` asked the splitter for almost no width, so the right column got what was left (about 420 px at 150 %), while its content's minimum is set by the clinician radios (a radio never wraps; each quotes up to 90 characters) and the Generate group's two non-wrapping fixed lines. The content was laid out wider than the viewport, so the consent paragraph wrapped at that width and was cut, and the stretched combo boxes ran to it.
  - THE FIX: `ui/review.py` `_ColumnScroll` — `right_scroll` asks for its content's minimum width plus frame and a vertical bar's width, and re-asks the splitter on every re-layout of the content (`LayoutRequest` through the filter `setWidget` installs). Its height asks for nothing more, so S.2's vertical behaviour is unchanged, and the horizontal bar stays "as needed" as an honest fallback. `ui/transcript.py` `_WrappingLine(QLabel)` for the legend and the group's two fixed lines: they word-wrap (minimum = widest word) but still ASK for their one-line size. `QSplitter` shares the width out by its children's size hints, so a plain word-wrapped label would have moved the handle at 100 % too. The handle moves only where a radio would otherwise run past its pane. Named limit: a radio still cannot narrow, so near the 960-px minimum window with a long quote both minimums may not fit (not measured). No construction moved; `test_shadow_exits.py` unchanged.

The `sendEvent` route is dropped as unfaithful, and S.5 is a MUST-PAUSE for the practitioner's re-check (step 6). The evidence for "unfaithful": (1) the route a physical mouse takes — real platform input — opens the menu under BOTH triggers, including Windows' release trigger; (2) the misdelivery appears only when the press SKIPS Qt's platform handling AND the menu is made at the press. The same injected press with the release trigger lands correctly. The most consistent reading, from the log and not from Qt's source, which is not in this repository: the made-up event takes its position from pointer state that only the platform handling updates before delivery — and that delivering the press updates afterwards, which is why the release case is right. A real click never skips that handling. NOT ruled out, and the reason this is a must-pause, not "no bug": a Windows-only route — Windows' own context-menu message carries a screen position, and display scaling changes how it maps back — which no offscreen test can exercise. **PR-MED-001 — [MED] The theme insertion point cannot reach the promised startup dialogs**
- Materiality: build-affecting
- Plan section challenged: D2; Task 1.2; Phase 1 smoke.
- Evidence: `.cursor/plans/plan-ui-refresh.md:135`, `:214`, `:219`; `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/app.py:362`, `:367`, `:752`.
- Defect: Both pre-window warning boxes run inside refusal branches that return by line 760. Applying the theme after those branches cannot theme either box, contrary to D2 and the second-launch smoke expectation.
- Triage: Include in plan
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: The prescribed implementation cannot satisfy its own visual acceptance check.
- Current behaviour: Instance acquisition is followed immediately by the two refusal branches; successful startup continues after them.
- Desired behaviour: Theme ordering and the refusal-dialog acceptance criteria agree.
- Concrete amendment: “Call `theme.ensure_applied(app)` immediately after `acquire_instance_exclusion()` returns, before branching on its result. Theme application may run in a refused process; controller construction, sweeps and other shared-data work remain behind successful acquisition. The earlier not-installed path remains unchanged.”
- Pattern to follow: Preserve the existing guard before shared-data work.
- Pattern siblings: Both `_show_already_running_warning` and `_show_cannot_start_warning`.
- Invariant: A refused instance does not construct a controller or operate on session stores.
- Verification: Add startup-order coverage for acquired, already-running and cannot-start outcomes; retain the practitioner’s second-launch smoke.
- Regression risk: Startup tests must accommodate the theme call on refusal paths.
- /fix decision: Applied as a plan amendment
- /fix notes: D2 and Task 1.2: `ensure_applied` moved to the line after `QApplication([])` (`:747`), before `acquire_instance_exclusion()`; the guard's invariant restated as shared-data only; one startup-order test case per guard outcome added to Task 1.2
- /fix date: 2026-10-09
- /fix applied by: the orchestrating session (Fable 5.1), verified against the code first

#### MED-001: desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/theme.py:598 — the theme work behind the suite's 3x time was not bounded
- Classification: round 1 (none)
- Triage: Fix-now (structural part); the residual is measured next, not hidden
- Why it matters: a 3x suite (11:33 → 35:10) costs every later phase and run. `test_ui_screens.py` alone is 1.5x, so part of the growth comes from the size of the whole session, not from one module.
- Structural causes found and fixed:
  - (a) Every application-wide setter (`setStyle`, `setPalette`, `setFont`, `setStyleSheet`) restyles EVERY live widget in the session, and earlier modules' leaked windows stay alive. `test_ui_theme.py` re-ran the full apply about nine times late in the run: the Fusion test 3, the dark test 3, and each of the 3 start-up cases cleared the marker, so `main()` re-applied with `setStyle` again. Now `apply_theme` skips any setter whose value the app already has (`app.palette() != …`, `app.font() != …`, `app.styleSheet() != …`; a scheme change still sets the palette and the sheet). The Fusion test re-applies the CURRENT scheme, so only `setStyle` runs. The dark test does two full applies (the flip and the way back). The start-up cases stub `apply_theme` with a stamping stub (the real one is `TestApply`'s). About nine full restyles become one `setStyle` and two scheme flips.
  - (b) `set_role` / `set_variant` / `mark_card` repolished widgets still being built. Now `_repolish` (`:656`) returns early unless `WA_WState_Polished` is set; Qt polishes the widget, property included, before it is first shown.
  - (c) `render_qss` rebuilt its ~100 rules on every call; now `functools.cache` (Tokens is frozen and hashable).
  - (d) The scheme-follow slot was a lambda closing over the application. Now a module function `_on_scheme_changed` (`:638`) looks the application up, so nothing can pin it alive.
- Not changed: the per-widget cost of an application stylesheet itself (Qt matches every widget against the sheet's rules at polish, and draws it through the sheet). That is inherent to D2 and is what the 1.5x on `test_ui_screens.py` alone most likely measures.
- Residual: to be measured by the composer's full run with `--durations=30`. If it stays well above the single-module 1.5x, the next candidate is leaked shown windows and their timers being restyled and repainted. That cost exists unthemed too, and only grows with the sheet. Any fix there changes the test harness (closing windows per test), so it goes to the practitioner as a decision, not a silent fix.
- Verification: `test_a_repeat_of_the_same_scheme_restyles_nothing` (none of the four setters runs on a same-scheme apply); `test_a_scheme_change_applies_to_the_current_application`; the composer's timing run.
- Regression risk: `apply_theme` callers (`ensure_applied`, `_on_scheme_changed`); a palette compare that says "equal" wrongly would skip a needed set. Qt's `QPalette ==` compares every colour group's brushes, so a scheme change (different colours) always sets.
- /fix decision: Applied (structural part); residual pending the composer's timing run
- /fix date: 2026-10-16

#### MED-002: desktop/tests/test_integration_no_sockets.py:385 — the no-sockets children no longer prove "what ships"
- Classification: round 1 (none)
- Triage: Fix-now
- Why it matters: these children re-create `app.main()`'s start-up under the socket guard ("the child proves what SHIPS"). `main()` now applies the theme (Fusion, the font database, the stylesheet) on the line after the application. The offline contract's proof (no connection at start-up or idle) did not cover that step.
- Current behaviour: the three children (`:385` the start-up proof, `:522` and `:628` the pipe-relay apps) build an unthemed app.
- Desired behaviour: each applies the theme exactly where `main()` does.
- Pattern siblings (searched: `QApplication([])` inside a string in `tests/`): the three here, fixed; `test_hands_free.py:759`, `test_system_pause.py:859` and `test_ui_pause_and_unreviewed.py:288` — native hotkey and power-event children. They assert message wiring, not start-up parity, and never run `main()`, so they keep an unthemed app; recorded under the review inputs below.
- Verification: the composer's full run (these legs run in it).
- /fix decision: Applied — `from scribe_desktop.ui import theme` and `theme.ensure_applied(app)` after each `app = QApplication([])`
- /fix date: 2026-10-16

#### LOW-003: desktop/tests/test_ui_theme.py:417 — the idempotency test's stub never stamped the marker
- The composer's one failure: `ensure_applied` is idempotent through the stamped scheme marker (D2), and the stub replaced the stamp. The TEST was wrong, not `ensure_applied`. The stub now stamps the marker as `apply_theme` does, and three calls apply once and connect once. — Triage: Fix-now; /fix decision: Applied

#### LOW-004: desktop/tests/test_ui_theme.py:282 — the "values live only in `ui/theme.py`" guard checked three spellings
- The text search found `#rrggbb`, `font-weight` and `setStyleSheet(` only. A named colour (`QColor("red")`), a per-widget `setFont` or a `setPalette` elsewhere would pass, and the design-system page claimed "a colour … anywhere else fails". This is the lessons' "derive the whole surface" rule.
- Now an AST test confines, to `ui/theme.py`, the imports of `QColor` / `QFont` / `QPalette` / `QBrush` / `QStyleFactory` and every `setStyleSheet` / `setPalette` / `setFont` / `setStyle` / `setForeground` / `setBackground` / `setAutoFillBackground` attribute. A grep found none outside the theme today. The page now says exactly what fails.
- Triage: Fix-now; /fix decision: Applied

#### LOW-005: desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/theme.py:351 — keyboard focus was invisible on danger buttons and lists
- The danger variant's red border out-ranked `QPushButton:focus`: in QSS a pseudo-state and an attribute rank alike, and the later rule wins. The list's `outline: 0px` removed Qt's item focus rectangle with no border change to replace it.
- Added `QPushButton[variant="danger"]:focus` and `QListWidget:focus`, both `focus`-coloured borders. `test_every_focusable_control_the_sheet_borders_shows_focus` pins every bordered focusable control. The page says so; the link variant shows focus by its colour.
- Triage: Fix-now; /fix decision: Applied

#### Review inputs — dispositions (no finding)
- **The five extra token fields** (`text_disabled`, `accent_hover`, `hover`, `pressed`, `rail_width`): conform. D1's rule is that one module owns every value. C13 requires disabled, hover and pressed states, and those need values. D1's role list was the planned minimum. The design-system "Tokens" section names all of them.
- **The outlined danger button**: conforms. D1 asks for a `danger` variant and leaves its look to the theme. Outlined reads in both schemes, where a filled `#f87171` with white text would not in dark. No screen uses the variant until Task 2.4, so the Phase 2 smoke is where it is first seen.
- **The six subprocess children that create their own app**: three are fixed (MED-002). The other three (`test_hands_free.py:759`, `test_system_pause.py:859`, `test_ui_pause_and_unreviewed.py:288`) conform. They check native-event wiring, never run `main()`, and assert nothing a style changes.
- **`test_shadow_exits.py` gained two lines per site** (the call and its local import), against the brief's "one line each". This conforms to the plan's intent: no pin or counter changed, and the import is the call's precondition.
- **22 module-scoped `qapp` fixtures, not 21**: the plan's count is a 2026-10-09 figure. The category (module fixtures the autouse fixture follows) is unchanged, and there is still no fifth in-body creator.

- **PR-LOW-001** (LOW, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/theme.py:257`): The primary button’s promised focus-border change is ineffective in light mode because its normal border and focus border have identical colours. — Evidence: LIGHT defines both `accent="#2563eb"` and `focus="#2563eb"`; the primary rule sets `f"border-color: {t.accent}"`, and its focus rule sets `f"border-color: {t.focus}"`. The selector-presence assertion in `test_ui_theme.py:168` cannot detect this. Recommendation: Fix-now — Give primary-button focus a distinct visual treatment in both schemes and verify that it differs from the unfocused treatment. /fix decision: Applied
  - The primary focus border is now `text`, distinct from `accent` in both schemes.
  - Pattern sibling found and fixed in the same pass: the link variant's focus colour `accent_hover` equalled its own `link` colour in light (`#1d4ed8`). The link now carries a transparent 1px border that turns `focus` on focus (so the size never changes), and its hover/pressed colour is `accent`.
  - New `test_a_focused_controls_border_differs_from_its_unfocused_border` compares, for every bordered focusable control in BOTH schemes, the parsed border colour of `X` and `X:focus`. It replaces the selector-presence check the peer named as blind.
  - Search evidence: every `:focus` rule in `render_qss` (9 controls).

- **PR-LOW-002** (LOW, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/theme.py:354`): A selected list item retains enabled selection colours when its list is disabled: the explicit item rule overrides the disabled palette, while the disabled rule styles only the list. This leaves the selected row looking actionable despite C13. — Evidence: `"QListWidget::item:selected"` sets `f"color: {t.on_accent}"` and `f"background-color: {t.accent}"`; the subsequent `"QListWidget:disabled"` rule has no corresponding item rule. Recommendation: Fix-now — Add a disabled selected-item rule using disabled colours and cover the combined selected-and-disabled state. /fix decision: Applied
  - New `QListWidget::item:selected:disabled` rule (`theme.py:372`): `text_disabled` on `border`.
  - Pattern sibling fixed in the same pass: the inputs' `selection-color` / `selection-background-color` also out-ranked the disabled palette. Their `:disabled` rule now sets both to the disabled colours.
  - The combo box popup and menu items need nothing: a disabled combo box opens no popup, and `QMenu::item:disabled` already comes after `:selected` at equal specificity.
  - New `test_a_disabled_control_keeps_no_enabled_selection_colours` (both schemes).

- **PR-LOW-003** (LOW, test-harness, `desktop/tests/test_ui_theme.py:377`): The scheme-switch test does not inject both schemes as C14 requires. Its return scheme comes from the existing application, so a dark-initialised run tests dark→dark→dark and never verifies a light transition. — Evidence: `saved = qapp.property(theme.SCHEME_PROPERTY)`, `current = saved if saved in ("light", "dark") else "light"`, followed by `theme.force_scheme("dark")` and restoration through `theme.apply_theme(qapp, current)`. Recommendation: Fix-now — Explicitly establish light through `force_scheme`, assert light→dark→light palette and stylesheet changes, then restore the prior state in `finally`. /fix decision: Applied
  - Folded into the suite-time rework below. `test_force_scheme_switches_light_dark_light` (`test_ui_theme.py:476`) injects light, then dark, then light through `force_scheme`, whatever the host reports. It asserts each switch's palette (`Window` colour), stylesheet, marker and announced tokens, and that the font is set once; `finally` resets the seam and the marker.

  - The live in-place restyle of an existing label (Flow 3) is now `test_an_existing_label_restyles_in_place_light_dark_light` (`:522`), on its own small widget tree.
  - `test_apply_sets_fusion_once_the_font_and_the_marker` uses the same fake surface, and now also pins D2's setter order.

#### LOW-001: desktop/tests/test_ui_review.py:421 — the saved row expected Write hidden for an unlinked note (the composer's one failure)
- The TEST was wrong, not the build. Evidence, from the code as it stands and Phase 2's diff (`git diff 77d950c -- note.py main_window.py`: no line of `_write_control`, `_apply_write_binding`, `models.write_control` or `MainWindow._live_write_binding` changed):
  - `MainWindow._live_write_binding` returns `WriteBinding(session_id, linked=False)` for ANY live, non-terminal session, linked or not — None only with no live session ("None with no live session: Write is hidden").
  - `NoteScreen._write_control` returns None only for no binding; `_apply_write_binding` then sets `write_button.setVisible(control is not None)`. So an unlinked live session's note shows Write, disabled, with `models.write_control`'s `unlinked` line on the button's tooltip and in `write_label`.
  - 0.3.3 pins exactly that on the Note tab: `test_ui_screens.py::…::test_an_unlinked_session_never_reads_the_record` (`not write_button.isEnabled()`, `write_label.text() == write_line("unlinked")`). Adoption calls no `show()` / `setVisible` and hides no ancestor of the button (the row's widget is the Review page itself).
- Fix: the saved row now asserts Write shown, disabled, its tooltip and `write_label` the `unlinked` line; the draft row gains the sibling check (shown, disabled, `not_saved`, the line visible on the Review page). The queued-no-note row's "hidden" stays right: no review has begun, so the Note engine has no binding.
- Pattern sibling (searched: "no Write", "Write hidden", `write_button.isHidden` in `test_ui_review.py` and the plan): the Phase 2 smoke script's step 7 said "there is no Write button (unlinked)" — reworded to the 0.3.3 behaviour (shown, disabled, the unlinked line; the disabled button is skipped by Tab).
